Also accepts a raw PDF or arXiv id/URL, which it converts to that same Markdown + assets layout first. Use this FIRST whenever the user wants to reproduce, audit, extract claims from, or summarize the methods of a paper — especially when they point at a .md file and an images/ folder, say "here's the markdown paper", or "reproduce this arXiv paper". - **paper-ingest**: Normalize `_raw/` into extracted chunks, `index.md`, and `summary.md`, and register the paper in `_index/`. ([L4pisLazuli/grounded-paper-skills](https://github.com/L4pisLazuli/grounded-paper-skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/paper-ingest))

Downloads files and updates .gitignore and README routing. Context only: writes no implementation code and sets up no project stack. ([oprogramadorreal/optimus-claude](https://github.com/oprogramadorreal/optimus-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-init) · ★74)
- **paper-ink-charts**: Render scientific figures — bar, line, scatter, box, heatmap, multi-panel — as warm-paper single-ink charts. Activate when producing figures for a paper, thesis, lab report, or poster; when a chart needs to read as typeset rather than as default matplotlib; when the user asks for 墨迹/暖纸/单墨/论文插图风格 or complains that a figure looks like default output; when replacing viridis/tab10 defaults, adding error bars, layering significance markers, or laying out shared-scale multi-panel comparisons. - **paper-innovation-analyst**: Use this skill when the user provides academic papers or extracted paper assets and needs structured technical reading, novelty assessment, limitation mining, implementable innovation ideas, experiment design, multi-paper comparison, reviewer-style critique, or evidence-aware analysis of formulas, tables, figures, references, scanned pages, and supplementary material. - **paper-intake**: Standardize conversations, experiment notes, result files, code paths, figures, videos, venue constraints, and known literature into paper_input.md 2.0. - **paper-intake**: Stage 1 of the PaperForge pipeline. Reads a paper's brief.md and inventories sources/, classifies every source file (prior literature, the author's own experimental data, the author's own code, notes, or a style/reference exemplar), and determines whether this is a paper reporting the author's own results or a review/survey/position paper. Use when starting a new paper (after /paperforge:paper-new) or when sources/ has changed and needs re-classification. ([AnasSAV/AI-research-paper-writer](https://github.com/AnasSAV/AI-research-paper-writer/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-intake))
- **paper-intake**: Take a research paper PDF into references/. Use whenever a new article is added to this repo, or an existing one needs re-processing. - **paper-intake**: Structured intake interview for an academic paper project (robotics/ML). Use whenever the author starts a new paper, says "start the intake", "new paper", "let's write up &lt;project&gt;", "interview me", or whenever state/interview.md is empty or thin and drafting is about to begin. Also use to run a focused mini-intake before drafting a specific section whose intent is not yet covered. The interview replaces ad-hoc prompt engineering: it is how the agent takes the initiative to fully understand the research, the author's line of thinking, and writing intent. - **paper-integrity**: 논문·기술 보고서 작성 정직성(integrity) 게이트. CLAUDE.md §11을 실행 가능한 스킬로 구현. 수치 출처 검증, 데이터 출처 구분(시뮬/합성/실측/이론), 미수행 항목 정직 disclosure, 강한 표현(first/comprehensive 등) 차단, self-plagiarism 회피, BibTeX 1:1 매칭, 그리고 done 마킹 전 §11.8 빌드+검증 grep 게이트를 verify.sh로 실행. Triggers: 논문 검증, 논문 정직성, paper integrity, paper verify, done 게이트, 검증 게이트, 논문 done 체크, bib 정합성, abstract 단어수, 페이지 한도, integrity check, 논문 빌드 검증, reject 위험, 과장 표현 점검. NOT FOR: 논문 내용/문장 작성 자체(별도), 한국 학회 워크플로우(kr-conference-paper 사용), PDF 변환(make-pdf), 단행본 빌드(book-builder), 연구계획서(plan-research). - **paper-interpret**: 单篇学术论文的固定解读框架：输出 5 块结构（这篇在问什么 / 研究设计 / 关键结果 / 局限 / 对课题参考），研究设计每条附「对应 RQ 的解释」。适用于解读单篇论文、或综述流程中的逐篇解读 环节。触发词：解读这篇论文、这篇论文讲了什么、interpret、按框架解读。 ([zeros335882878/dsh-paper-survey](https://github.com/zeros335882878/dsh-paper-survey/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-interpret))

- **paper-jiedu-latex**: 把一篇论文做成 LaTeX 中文解读 PDF。本地 PDF / arXiv 取原文 → MinerU 全文解析 → 深读 → 填固化 LaTeX 模板(ctexart+fandol，含 keybox / 图块宏 / 我的评价结构)→ xelatex 出 PDF。硬规则:原文关键图必须嵌入，自画示意图只能补充不能替代。当用户要把某篇论文 / PDF 做成"解读 PDF / 论文解读 / LaTeX 解读"，或要一份可打印分享的论文导读 PDF 时用。 ([tianhanz/paper-jiedu-latex](https://github.com/tianhanz/paper-jiedu-latex/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/paper-jiedu-latex) · ★9 · 2 copies)

- **paper-trade**: 仮想資金でペーパートレード（売買練習）を行う。実 API は public ticker と 1m candles のみで、private/trade エンドポイントには触れない。 代表トリガー: 「BTC を仮想で買って」「ペーパー口座の残高見て」 「ペーパーの損益見せて」「仮想口座リセットして」 注意: 実発注（`bitbank trade ...`）は対象外。状態は `~/.bitbank/paper-state.json` にローカル保存される。 ([bitbankinc/bitbank-lab-cli](https://github.com/bitbankinc/bitbank-lab-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-trade) · ★4)
- **paper-trade-runner**: Runs a signal-only paper-trading loop for Indian markets — generates entry signals, simulates fills at LTP, manages stop-loss and target, resolves at EOD, and persists trade history. Never places real orders. Trigger when user wants to paper trade, backtest live, simulate a strategy, or generate intraday signals without risk. ([rahulcommercial/claude-code-for-indian-traders](https://github.com/rahulcommercial/claude-code-for-indian-traders/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-trade-runner) · ★2)

- **paper-trading**: Run a paper-trading workflow over analyzed symbols. Use when user wants the live-verification stage in simulated form: trade rehearsal, ledger updates, A-share settlement rules, and explicit monitoring of what the local paper book does and does not model. ([NatsuFox/A-Stockit](https://github.com/NatsuFox/A-Stockit/tree/HEAD/skills/astockit/paper-trading) · ★3)
- **paper-trading**: Paper-trading ledger rules for equity-analyst — read the paper portfolio from a local ledger file instead of a broker, simulate the portfolio manager's or index-fund advisor's decisions with Montrose fees, and update the ledger, trade log and performance log. Use only when /analyze or /index-funds is run in paper mode; never for real accounts. ([joaroo/equity-analyst](https://github.com/joaroo/equity-analyst/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-trading))
- **paper-trans**: Format-preserving academic paper PDF translation to Korean. Overlay method: original text blocks are redacted in place (figures, equations, line art preserved) and Korean is inserted into the same bbox, so page count and layout stay identical to the source paper. Input: local PDF path or URL (arxiv.org/abs links auto-convert to /pdf). Claude translates directly (no external MT API); papers over 10 pages use parallel subagent translation. Output: KO_&lt;name&gt;.pdf plus verification. Trigger: "paper translate", "translate paper", "paper-trans", "논문 번역", "arxiv 번역", "페이퍼 번역", "translate this paper t… ([jesamkim/oh-my-skills](https://github.com/jesamkim/oh-my-skills/tree/HEAD/my-skills/paper-trans) · ★1)
- **paper-translate**: 翻譯論文 PDF（預設英文翻台灣繁中）：保留原版面的 PDF（只有譯文或原文並排），或「左譯文、右原文整頁」並可在網頁右側問 AI 的閱讀頁（閱讀頁另支援簡中、日、韓、德、西、法文）。使用者要「翻譯這篇論文／這個 PDF」「做中英對照 PDF」「把 Zotero 裡這篇翻成中文」時使用。不用於純文字翻譯、摘要或導讀。 ([minjunnzheng/paper-pdf-translator](https://github.com/minjunnzheng/paper-pdf-translator/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-translate))
- **paper-translate-advisor-feedback**: 当用户拿来导师的录音转写、便条、口头反馈、批注、邮件意见时， 禁止直接喂 AI 改文章；必须先把口语化 / 模糊 / 隐含的反馈翻译为 「位置 + 改什么 + 改成什么 + 标准 + 不动哪里」的可执行指令清单，让用户确认后再开始改。 Use when 用户提供导师反馈（录音、批注、便条、邮件、聊天截图、口述）， 并要求"按导师意见改一下"。 ([Lambenthan/paper-discipline-skills](https://github.com/Lambenthan/paper-discipline-skills/tree/HEAD/paper-translate-advisor-feedback) · ★19)
- **paper-translate-ja**: 英語の学術論文PDFを日本語化して理解補助するスキル。原文ページ画像と日本語解説を左右対比HTMLで生成する。要約+キー箇所(定義・図表キャプション・数値結果・結論)の忠実翻訳という方針で、全文逐語翻訳は行わない。ユーザーが「論文を日本語訳して」「英語論文を読みやすくしたい」「勉強会用にPDFをまとめて」「arXiv論文を翻訳」「学会論文の日本語版がほしい」「論文読解を手伝って」のような依頼をしたとき、または .pdf を渡されて翻訳・解説を求められたとき、必ずこのスキルを使うこと。 ([doiken/dotfiles](https://github.com/doiken/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/paper-translate-ja))
- **paper-translate-summary-zh**: 当用户希望将一篇研究论文翻译为中文并进行深度总结时使用这个skill。触发场景包括：用户提供PDF文件、arXiv链接、论文URL、或直接说"翻译这篇论文"、"帮我翻译+总结这篇paper"、"把这篇论文翻成中文"等。目标是产出两份产物：（1）完整中文翻译，保留原文排版结构、公式、图片引用和专业术语；（2）基于翻译内容的深度中文总结，遵循论文深度阅读skill的结构。适用于所有学术领域。 ([3550625279-ux/LLM-wiki-Vault](https://github.com/3550625279-ux/LLM-wiki-Vault/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/paper-translate-summary-zh) · ★1)
